Projected gradient algorithm:
- Given h, compute C, M.
- Active lags: top K with C close to M (e.g., within tol 1e-4 or threshold). Weights uniform or based on gap: w_l = exp((C_l-M)/s)/sum.
- Compute gradient G = ∑ w_l grad C_l. For each active lag m, gradient vector can be computed vectorized:
   For lag m>0:
     g[i] += 1-h[i+m] for i=0..n-1-m
     g[i] += -h[i-m] for i=m..n-1
   For lag m<0 similarly. This is O(n) per lag.
- Since sum constraint: subtract mean(G) to enforce zero-sum direction. Boundary projection: for step size lr, candidate = clip(h - lr G, 0,1), then project sum to n/2 (maintain). But clipping may break sum; use projection. Then evaluate exact C. Use line search: try lr values, accept if max improves.
- This can do global moves; pair local search refines.

Need efficient projection maintaining bounds and sum. For h - lr G, we can use isotonic? Simple iterative relaxation: clip, adjust sum by distributing to variables not at bounds. O(iterations n). For n 500-2000 okay. Better implement exact Euclidean projection onto box+sum using bisection on threshold: Need minimize ∑(x_i - y_i)^2 s.t. 0≤x≤1, sum=S. Solution x_i = clip(y_i - τ,0,1). Find τ such that sum=S. Bisection 60 iterations O(n) each; good. This preserves [0,1] exactly. Use for all projection.

But sum of h after projection exactly S. Good.

g = zeros(n)
if m >=0:
  if m==0:
    g += 1 - 2h
  else:
    # first factor i=0..n-1-m
    g[0:n-m] += 1 - h[m:n]
    # second factor i=m..n-1; term h_{i-m}(1-h_i) derivative -h_{i-m}
    g[m:n] -= h[0:n-m]
if m<0:
  p=-m

  # second factor i-m valid => i+p valid => i=0..n-1-p: g[0:n-p] -= h[p:n]

The projected x = clip(y - tau, 0, 1). As tau increases, sum decreases. Range tau in [min(y)-1? max(y)?]. Use lo = min(y) - 1, hi = max(y)+1 maybe. Then fix residual: diff=target-x.sum(); if abs>... adjust a non-bound variable with room. Choose idx where x>0 and x<1 (or closest to target?). Add diff to one variable? If diff positive and residual >1-x? But due bisection should be small. But this may violate sum due clip. We can do a final correction by setting one variable: choose i with not at bound (or largest x if diff<0) and `x[i] += diff` within clip. Since diff ~1e-14, okay. - `correlate_fft(h, v=None)`: If v None, v=1-h. Return raw length 2n-1. Need ensure no negative zeros. Use L=1<<(2*n-1).bit_length(). If n large (e.g., 5000, L=16384) fine. rfft memory okay.
```
def full_corr(h, v=None):
    n=len(h); L=1; while L<2*n-1: L*=2
    if v is None: v=1.0-h
    H=np.fft.rfft(h,L); V=np.fft.rfft(v[::-1],L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(H*V,L, n=2*n-1)
    return out
```
But `irfft` with n parameter? signature irfft(a,n=None). We can do `np.fft.irfft(H*V, L)[:2*n-1]`. Need v[::-1] copy. For small n, np.correlate maybe easier but FFT fine.
Potential issue: For h real, output may be floating with tiny imaginary no; irfft returns real. Good.

def apply_point_delta(h, c_raw, p, delta):
    if delta ==0: return
    # first factor: lags m=0..n-1-p, idx n-1+m
    n=len(h)
    # old h before change
    if n-1-p >=0:
       m_slice = slice(0, n-p) # length n-p? Actually m=0..n-1-p inclusive count n-p

       # h[p:p+n-p] valid; includes h[p] for m=0

       # want add -delta*h[i] for i=0..p

Pair transfer function:
```
def apply_transfer(h,c,p,q,amount): # h_p -= amount, h_q += amount
   if p==q or amount==0: return
   apply_point_delta(h,c,p,-amount)
   apply_point_delta(h,c,q,amount)
```
Sum unchanged.

Local search loop:
```
def local_search(h,c,rng,deadline, max_iter, step_base=0.05, active_K=6, target_c5=None):
   n=len(h); M=np.max(c); best=M; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); iters_since=0
   while time<deadline:
      active = top K indices
      improved=False
      # Active-pair moves
      for _ in range(num_candidates_round):
         choose lag idx from active (weighted random)
         m=idx-(n-1); if m==0: choose random pair? lag0 gradient is individual 1-2h; no pair? Lag0 C0=∑h(1-h), to reduce, move mass toward 0/1: choose p high mid, q zero? Pair transfer? For m=0, C0 change for point p: delta*(1-2h_p). Moving from p to q changes C0 = -d(1-2h_p)+d(1-2h_q)=d(1+h_p -h_q? actually (1-2hq)-(1-2hp)=2(hp-hq)). If hp>hq, moving p->q decreases C0 (d positive gives? ΔC0 = 2d(hq-hp) negative). So pair high->low reduces C0. For m=0, any pair works.
         if m !=0:
            mabs=abs(m)
            if mabs>=n: continue
            if m>0: i in 0..n-1-m; p=i,q=i+m. For positive lag, reduce by moving p->q if h_p>h_q, else q->p if h_q>h_p.
            if m<0: Use pair i=q? For lag -mabs, term h_j(1-h_{j-mabs}). To reduce, move high to low among pair (j,H?) Let p = i+m if? If m<0, let a = i+m? simpler choose i in 0..n-1-mabs, pair (i,i+mabs). For negative lag, term at upper index i+mabs? C_{-p}=∑_{i=p}^{n-1} h_i(1-h_{i-p}). For pair (i, i+p), high at i+p and low at i? term h_{i+p}(1-h_i). To reduce, move from high to low: if h[i+p]>h[i], donor=i+p, receiver=i; else donor=i, receiver=i+p? Actually if h_i high and h_{i+p} low, term for negative lag? h_i is second factor for pair ending at i? The penalized pair is (lower,upper): upper h(1-lower). If lower high and upper low, C negative has term low? h_upper(1-h_lower) small if upper low; not penalized. To reduce, we want upper high, lower low -> move lower to upper. So for negative lag, move from i to i+mabs if h_i<h? Let's rely on actual e signs; we can just choose direction based on h difference and test both? For a pair separated by mabs, possible moves p->q or q->p. We can try whichever (high to low) and accept if improves; if not tried? Could try low to high occasionally.
         donor, receiver = ...
         amount = step * min(h[donor], 1-h[receiver])? Actually amount max = min(h_donor,1-h_recv). Use maybe fraction of gap: if h_donor and h_recv, moving toward equality amount = min(step, (h_donor - h_recv)/2) if donor>recv; else if low>high? We'll compute.
         apply, newM=np.max(c); if newM < M - tol: accept; update M, best, improved; else inverse.
      # Random pair moves: choose random p,q, direction high->low or random sign; small step.
      if not improved after some rounds: perturb h? or break.
```
Need avoid O(active K*n) candidate generation each round. We can for each active lag choose random i each candidate. Number candidates per sweep maybe 1000-5000 depending n.

Step size: h values in [0,1]. For fine improvements near 0.3808, need step maybe 1e-3 or 1e-4. For global, step 0.05. We can run multiple step scales: large 0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.002,0.0005,1e-4. For each scale, do fixed number of candidates or time. Accept only exact max improvement (steepest ascent). This is fast but may miss moves that temporarily increase max to lower after multiple steps. We can add simulated annealing: accept if newM < M + T; but since we need exact max, SA can escape. However accepting increases worst lag, may need return. Use small T.

Since we can do many evals, maybe use SA with incremental C: Accept if newM < M or (random < exp(-(newM-M)/T)). But C raw improvements ~ maybe 1e-6; T raw? dx? We can use scaled C for temperature. Let objective val M*dx. T in C5 units e.g., 1e-4 to 1e-6. If newC5 < oldC5 or T>0 and prob. When accept worse, M increases; need update. This can escape local minima. But to ensure best, keep best_h.

Let's design local search in detail with vectorized operations for performance.

Candidate apply and test function:
```
def try_move(h,c,p,q,d, tol=1e-15):
    if p==q or d==0: return False, np.max(c)
    max_d_for_pos = min(h[p], 1.0-h[q])
    min_d_for_pos = -min(h[q], 1.0-h[p])
    if d>max_d... d=max; if d<min...
    if abs(d)<1e-16: return False
    apply_transfer(h,c,p,q,d)
    M=np.max(c)
    hp_copy? if reject: apply_transfer(h,c,q,p,d) # equivalently -d
    return M
```

So full_corr:
```
def full_corr(h, v=None):
    n=len(h)
    if v is None: v=1.0-h
    L=1
    m=2*n-1
    while L<m: L<<=1
    H=np.fft.fft(h,L); V=np.fft.fft(v,L)
    r=np.fft.ifft(H*np.conj(V)).real
    out=np.empty(2*n-1)
    out[:n]=r[L-(n-1):]
    out[n:]=r[:n]
    # small clean
    out[np.abs(out)<1e-14]=0
    return out
```

Indices output k=0..2n-2 correspond lag = k-(n-1).
For lag -q (q=n-1..1): output idx=(n-1)-q = n-1-q. We want r[L-q]. So for q=n-1 down to1: idx 0..n-2 get r[L-(n-1)]..r[L-1]. Then idx n-1 (lag0)=r[0]. Positive lag q=1..n-1: idx n-1+q get r[q]. So output length: first n-1 = r[L-(n-1):L] (length n-1), then n = r[:n]. Thus:
```
out = np.empty(2*n-1)
out[:n-1] = r[L-(n-1):]  # length n-1
out[n-1:] = r[:n]
```

Incremental update details in numpy slices:
```
def apply_point_delta(h, c, p, delta):
    if delta == 0.0: return
    n=len(h)
    # first: m=0..n-1-p, size n-p
    end = n - p  # count
    # c indices n-1 : n-1+end
    c[n-1:n-1+end] += delta * (1.0 - h[p:p+end])
    # second: size p+1, indices n-1+p down to n-1
    # using slice c[n-1+p : n-2 : -1]
    # if p=0: c[n-1:n-2:-1]
    c[n-1+p:n-2:-1] += -delta * h[p::-1]
    h[p] += delta
    if h[p] < 0: h[p]=0.0 elif >1:1
```
But note: first slice `c[n-1:n-1+end]` for p=0,end=n => indices n-1 to 2n-2 okay. For p>0, end=n-p, end index n-1+n-p = 2n-p-2 ≤2n-2. Good.
Second slice: `c[n-1+p:n-2:-1]` requires start > n-2. For p=0 start=n-1, stop=n-2, step -1 works. For p=n-1 start=2n-2, stop=n-2, length n? indexes 2n-2 down to n-1, yes. Good. But if n=1? stop -? ignore.
Need ensure adding arrays lengths match slice lengths. First length end. `h[p:p+end]` length end. Second length p+1, `h[p::-1]` length p+1. Good.

But applying two deltas: after first, h[p] changed. For second point q, if q was within h slice for first update? The c array already exact for containing p change. Coefficients for q use current h. Fine.

Potential issue when applying transfer h_p-=d,h_q+=d where p and q slices overlap (same lag terms involving both). Sequential exact. But when applying point delta q +d, first slot uses `1-h[q+m]`; if q+m=p maybe uses h_p low, correct. second uses `-h[q-m]`; if q-m=p, uses h_p low, correct. Good.

Local deterministic sweep implementation more concrete:
```
def sweep_improvements(h, c, deadline, step, rng, K=6, min_improve=1e-12, max_sweeps=50):
    n=len(h); target=n*0.5
    M=float(np.max(c)); best_M=M; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
    sweeps=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       sweeps +=1
       improved=False
       c_copy? Need active recompute after each lag? We'll use current c.

Simulated annealing with incremental:
```
def sa_local(h,c,deadline,rng,T0=1e-4,Tmin=1e-7,cool=0.9995):
   n=len(h); M=np.max(c); best=M; best_h=c copy
   T=T0
   while time<deadline and T>Tmin:
      # generate move: active-lag pair or random pair
      # amount = random in [0, maxd], direction high-low or random
      # apply, newM; newC5=(newM-M)*dx
      if newM < M or random < exp(-(newM-M)/ (T/dx)?). Use raw: temp_raw = T / dx. Accept if delta_raw < temp_raw or exp(-delta_raw/temp_raw).
      update M, if newM<best best...
      T*=cool
      if no improve for many: perturb.
```
Temperature in C5 units. Since M raw differences are M_raw*dx. Use `Tc` (C5 units). Accept iff newC5-oldC5 <=0 or `rng.random() < exp(-(newC5-oldC5)/Tc)`.

Subgradient polish code:
```
def subgradient_step(h,c,deadline, steps=[...], K=8):
   n=len(h); target=n/2; M=np.max(c)
   active top K
   weights: if all close, 1/K; else softmax with scale = max(1e-6, (M - second_max?) maybe). Use top K.
   G=zeros
   for idx,w: G += w*grad_lag(h, idx-(n-1))
   G -= G.mean()
   # maybe normalize gradient: gn = np.linalg.norm(G); scale steps by 1/(gn+1e-8)?
   for step in base_steps:
      y=project_box_sum(h - step*G, target)
      cnew=full_corr(y)
      Mnew=max(cnew)
      if Mnew < M - tol:
          return y,cnew,Mnew,True
   return None
```
Gradient raw; step scale: The gradient sum over top K raw. For n=500, G components maybe O(K). If step=0.01, h change 0.01*10=0.1. Good. Use steps [0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002]. If no accept, stop. This can be called periodically after hill climb.

B_i = ∑_{m: i+m valid} W_m h_{i+m}
C_i = ∑_{m: i-m valid} W_m h_{i-m}

Pseudo:
```
def local_search_core(h0, deadline, rng, step_schedule=None, K=6, sa_budget=0.0):
    n=len(h0)
    h=project_box_sum(h0,n*0.5); c=full_corr(h); M=max(c)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_M=M
    if step_schedule is None: step_schedule=[0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.0001,0.00005,0.00002]
    # We don't want too many scales if tight budget. Use maybe first 8 for fast, last if time.
    for step in step_schedule:
       if time > deadline: break
       t_step = min(deadline, time + time_per_step(n))
       h,c,M = sweep_step(h,c,M,t_step,step,rng,K)
       if M<best_M: update
       # After each step, try subgradient if time
       if time < deadline - 5:
          for _ in range(20): step success...
    # SA if budget
    return best_h,best_c,best_M
```

Let's implement `attempt_pair(h,c, donor, receiver, amount, tol)`:
```
def test_move(h,c, donor, receiver, d):
    if d<=1e-15: return None
    maxd_pos = min(h[donor], 1.0-h[receiver])
    maxd_neg = -min(h[receiver], 1.0-h[donor])
    if d > maxd_pos: d = maxd_pos
    if d < maxd_neg: d = maxd_neg
    if abs(d)<1e-15: return None
    apply_transfer(h,c,donor,receiver,d)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    return M, d
```

```
def clip_transfer_amount(h,donor,receiver,d):
  pos=max(0,min(h[donor],1-h[receiver]))
  neg=-max(0,min(h[receiver],1-h[donor]))
  if d>pos: d=pos
  elif d<neg: d=neg
  return d
```

Pseudo `sweep_step`:
```
def sweep_step(h,c,M,deadline,step,rng,K=6,max_passes=200):
  n=len(h); tol=1e-12; best=(h.copy(),c.copy(),M)
  for pass in range(max_passes):
    if time>deadline: break
    improved=False
    active = top_k(c,K)
    # maybe sort by c descending
    for idx in active:
      lag=idx-(n-1)
      if lag==0:
         # do high-low pairs count maybe n//2 random or systematic? We'll do a limited number to not dominate.
         # Could select pairs by C0 gradient: pairs with large h diff. But random okay.
         count = min(1000, n*n//10?) too high. Simpler skip lag0 in structured sweep? C0 usually not max for binary, but for continuous maybe. If lag0 in top K, need handle.
         # For lag0, perform coordinate moves: for i where h_i>0.5 high, j where h_j<0.5 low, move high to low. We can choose random 100 pairs.
         for _ in range(min(200, n)):
           p=rng.randint(n); q=rng.randint(n)
           if h[p]>h[q]: d=p;r=q else d=q;r=p
           if abs<1e-8 continue
           maxd=min(h[d],1-h[r]); amt=min(step,maxd); test...
         continue
      ma=abs(lag)
      count=n-ma
      if count<=0: continue
      # order: deterministic ascending for reproducibility; maybe if step small use random? Use ascending.
      for i in range(count):
        if lag>0: d_i=i; r_i=i+ma else d_i=i+ma; r_i=i
        maxd=min(h[d_i],1.0-h[r_i]); amt=min(step,maxd)
        if amt<1e-12: continue
        apply; newM=max; if newM < M - tol: accept improved=True else revert
      # optional recompute active after each lag if many improvements
    if not improved: break
    # if M improved significantly, update best
  return h,c,M
```

Then transfer d->r changes C_m by `g_r - g_d` (since h_r +1, h_d -1). To reduce C_m (global max), need e = g_r - g_d <=0 for active max lags. We can compute g_p for p and lag m in O(1):
```
def point_coeff(p, m, h):
   val=0.0
   # first: if 0 <= p+m < n: val += 1 - h[p+m]
   # second: if 0 <= p-m < n: val += -h[p-m]
   return val
```

Helper `point_coeff_vec(pos, m, h)`:

n=len(h); g=zeros(len(pos))

```

`point_coeff_vec` can be optimized for scalar lag using slices:
For pos array D, R consecutive. But generic okay. To avoid creating arrays repeatedly inside active loop, for each primary lag we create D,R. For top K loop, each point_coeff_vec allocates g, idx arrays. Could be overhead. We can derive e for pair D/R and active lag m directly vectorized:
`e_i = point_coeff(R,m) - point_coeff(D,m)`.
We can implement a helper:
```
def transfer_e_vec(D,R,m,h):
   n=len(h); e=zeros(len(D))
   # g_R:
   idxR1=R+m; valid=(idxR1>=0)&(idxR1<n); e[valid] += 1-h[idxR1[valid]]
   idxR2=R-m; valid=(idxR2>=0)&(idxR2<n); e[valid] -= h[idxR2[valid]]
   # minus g_D:
   idxD1=D+m; valid; e[valid] -= (1-h[idxD1[valid]])
   idxD2=D-m; valid; e[valid] += h[idxD2[valid]]
   return e
```
This directly gives g_R - g_D. Four vector operations per active. For r/D ranges consecutive, can use slices and np.where but okay.

However, if D and R lengths count, and m various, many valid masks. For n=500, trivial. For n=1000, K=8, 32 vector ops per primary lag; 8 primaries ->256 vector ops per pass, okay.

Maybe simpler: For exact candidate test, use apply_transfer. For filter, use this vector e.

Need be careful with e for active lag at lag0: For D/R same set, e=2(h_D - h_R)? Our formula gives g_R-g_D with m=0: g=1-2h. g_R-g_D=2h_D-2h_R? Wait g_R - g_D = (1-2h_R)-(1-2h_D)=2(h_D-h_R). Earlier for transfer D->R, change = d(e) where e=g_R - g_D. If D high, R low, e negative? h_D>h_R -> 2h_D-2h_R positive. That's opposite! Let's recalc transfer D->R: h_D += -d, h_R += +d. Change = -d*g_D + d*g_R = d(g_R-g_D) = d(e). For D high h_D=0.8,R low0.2: e=g_R-g_D=(1-0.4)-(1-1.6)=0.6 - (-0.6)=1.2 positive; change positive, but direct C0: D term 0.8*0.2=0.16 -> D after 0.7:0.21; R 0.2->0.3:0.21 total 0.42 from0.32 increase! I earlier thought high-to-low reduces C0; actually C0 = sum h(1-h) is concave, moving mass from high to low (making both closer to 0.5) increases C0. To minimize C0, we should move mass from low to high (make more extreme): direction R->D (low to high). Good. So for lag0, to reduce C0, direction is low-to-high (donor low, receiver high). Our formula e positive for high-to-low; need e≤0 means move from low D to high R? If D low,R high, e=2(h_D-h_R) negative. So lag0 primary direction should be low-to-high, not high-to-low. But if we always choose direction along lag? Lag0 has two directions; to reduce C0, send mass to extrema: from value <0.5 to >0.5. So primary lag0 D=lower-valued, R=higher-valued. Good. Need `grad_lag(h,m)` top-level returning zeros. Implement with slices:
```
def grad_lag(h,m):
 n=len(h); g=np.zeros(n)
 if m==0: g=1-2h
 elif m>0:
   g[:n-m] += 1 - h[m:]
   g[m:] -= h[:n-m]
 else:
   p=-m
   g[p:] += 1 - h[:n-p]
   g[:n-p] -= h[p:]
 return g
```
This is scaled raw; dx not included. Use raw.

Now, vectorized `transfer_e_vec` implementation performance:
```
def transfer_e_vec(D,R,m,h):
    e = np.zeros(len(D), dtype=float)
    nr = R + m
    v = (nr >=0) & (nr < len(h))
    if v.any(): e[v] += 1.0 - h[nr[v]]
    nr = R - m
    v = (nr >=0) & (nr < len(h))
    if v.any(): e[v] -= h[nr[v]]
    nd = D + m
    v = (nd >=0) & (nd < len(h))
    if v.any(): e[v] -= (1.0 - h[nd[v]])
    nd = D - m
    v = (nd >=0) & (nd < len(h))
    if v.any(): e[v] += h[nd[v]]
    return e
```
Allocates masks each. Fine.

But D,R arrays are int dtype. For large n, okay. We can precompute D,R using `np.arange`.

Implement `project_box_sum` carefully:
```
def project_box_sum(y, target_sum):
    y=np.asarray(y,float)
    n=y.size
    if n==0: return y.copy()
    # if sum already close and within bounds, just fix exact maybe
    y=np.clip(y,0,1)
    s=y.sum()
    diff=target_sum-s
    if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
       # final exact adjust
    else:
       lo = y.min() - 1.0
       hi = y.max() + 1.0
       for _ in range(70):
          mid=(lo+hi)/2
          x=np.clip(y-mid,0,1)
          sx=x.sum()
          if sx > target_sum:
              lo=mid
          else:
              hi=mid
       x=np.clip(y-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # final exact adjustment
    s=x.sum(); diff=target_sum-s
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        if diff > 0:
            idxs=np.where(x < 1.0)[0]
        else:
            idxs=np.where(x > 0.0)[0]
        if idxs.size ==0:
            idxs=np.arange(n)
        # distribute evenly over up to n
        # To keep within bounds, adjust one by one? Residual tiny.
        adj=diff/idxs.size
        x[idxs]+=adj
        x=np.clip(x,0,1)
        # if still diff, set one
        s=x.sum(); diff=target_sum-s
        if abs(diff)>1e-15:
            if diff>0: idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-x))
            else: idx=int(np.argmin(x))
            x[idx]=float(np.clip(x[idx]+diff,0,1))
    # As a last resort, adjust last element
    s=x.sum(); diff=target_sum-s
    if abs(diff)>0:
       # choose not at bound
       idx = int(np.argmax(x*(1-x))) maybe
       x[idx]=float(np.clip(x[idx]+diff,0,1))
    return x
```
The last resort might still not exact if clip clipped all, but target feasible. Since target between 0,n, there is bound room. Use bisection should give sum within 1e-12. Final adjustments tiny.

But bisection 70* n operations for each projection; in subgradient maybe many calls (hundreds) n=500 => 70*500*100=3.5M, fine. For SA no projection (moves preserve). For resample few.

Alternative fast projection: If diff small, distribute to nonbounds; but bisection okay.

`full_corr` uses FFT; for n small maybe overhead. For n<64, use np.correlate? But okay. Ensure L is power of 2: `L=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()`.

`full_corr` output may have small negative; for max not issue. But for filter e using h only, not c.

Now, evaluating direct final `compute_c5_direct`:
```
def compute_c5_direct(h):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c)*dx)
```
Need ensure c raw length. For n even. This is O(n^2). If n=800, 640k operations, fine. If called many times in multi-n maybe 20*640k=12M, fine.

```
def validate_corr():
   rng=np.random.RandomState(0); n=13; h=rng.rand(n); v=1-h
   a=np.correlate(h,v,'full'); b=full_corr_fft(h)
   return np.allclose(a,b,atol=1e-10)
```

r[L-2] = r[6] (L=8). Circular m=6 ≡ -2: ∑ h_i v_{i-2 mod8}. i=2 gives a2 v0? i? i=1 -> v7=0, i=0->v6=0 => a2 v0. Direct lag -2: terms i=0? h0 v_{-2}=0; i? For lag -2, ∑_{i=2}^{n-1} h_i v_{i-2} = a2 v0. Good.
r[L-1]=7 ≡ -1: i=1 a1v0 + i=2 a2v1. Direct lag -1: i=1 a1v0, i=2 a2v1. Good.
r0 lag0. r1: a0v1+a1v2. r2: a0v2. Direct. OK.

Let's detail `local_search_advanced`:
```
def local_search_advanced(h, deadline, rng, step_list=None, K=8, max_passes=100, do_subgrad=True, do_sa=False, sa_time=0):
    n=len(h); h=project_box_sum(h,n*0.5); c=recompute_raw(h); M=float(np.max(c))
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c_raw=c.copy(); best_M=M
    dx=2.0/n
    if step_list is None: step_list = [0.5,0.25,0.12,0.06,0.03,0.015,0.008,0.004,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002]
    for step in step_list:
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       # Determine time slice maybe until no improve
       h,c,M = sweep_step(h,c,M, deadline, step, rng, K=K, max_passes=max_passes)
       if M < best_M - 1e-12:
           best_M=M; best_h=h.copy(); best_c_raw=c.copy()
       # subgradient
       if do_subgrad and time.time() < deadline - 10:
           t_sub = min(deadline-5, time.time()+8) # small per step
           h,c,M = subgradient_pass(h,c,M,t_sub,rng,K=K,max_iters=50)
           if M < best_M: update
       # maybe if no SA and time
    # SA if do_sa
    if do_sa and time.time() < deadline - sa_time? Let caller pass.
    return best_h,best_c_raw,best_M
```
But `sweep_step` takes deadline and will run until no improvement or deadline. For each step, if it takes all deadline, later steps no time. We need limit per step. Use `step_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + step_time)`, where step_time depends on n and step size. For large steps maybe 30s, small 10s. Simpler: pass `max_passes` limited and no per-step deadline? But if a small step improves a tiny bit for 100 passes, may consume. We'll set `time_per_step = max(5, min(40, budget?))`. Now, SA implementation:
```
def simulated_annealing(h,c,dx,deadline,rng,T0=1e-4,Tmin=1e-7,cool=0.9995,K=8):
   n=len(h); h=h.copy(); c=c.copy(); M=max(c); best_M=M; best_h=h.copy()
   T=T0; no_improve=0
   while time<deadline and T>Tmin:
      # if no_improve > 2000: perturb best
      if no_improve > 3000:
         h = best_h + rng.normal(0, 0.02, n); h=project_box_sum(h,n*0.5); c=recompute_raw(h); M=max(c); no_improve=0; T=max(T,1e-5)
      active=top_active(c,K)
      r=rng.random()
      if r < 0.75:
         idx=active[rng.randint(len(active))]
         lag=idx-(n-1)
         if lag==0:
            # lag0 low->high random pairs
            cnt=1
            p=rng.randint(n); q=rng.randint(n); if equal skip
            if h[p] < h[q]: d_p=p;r_p=q else d_p=q;r_p=p
         else:
            ma=abs(lag); i=rng.randint(n-ma)
            if lag>0: d_p=i; r_p=i+ma
            else: d_p=i+ma; r_p=i
         maxd=min(h[d_p],1-h[r_p]); if maxd<=0 continue
         d = rng.uniform(0.0, maxd) * 0.5? For SA, random amount uniform(1e-4, maxd*0.5). Use `d = min(maxd, rng.uniform(0.001, 0.05))` maybe.
      else:
         p,r=random; choose direction random sign: if rng<0.5: d=p;rec=r else swap
         maxd=...
      apply; newM; deltaC=(newM-M)*dx
      accept if deltaC<=0 or (T>0 and rng.random()<exp(-deltaC/T))
      if accept: M=newM; if M<best_M: best... no_improve=0 else no_improve++ (even if worse)
      else: revert; no_improve++
      T*=cool
   return best_h,best_M
```
If accept worse, no_improve not update best. Good. Temperature raw: use C5 units. `exp` underflow guard: ratio=deltaC/T; if ratio<50. T in [1e-7,1e-4]. DeltaC could be e.g., 0.001, ratio 10000 no accept. Good.
Step amount: For structured, if maxd large, amount uniform(0, maxd*0.2)? Use `d = min(maxd, rng.uniform(0.0005, 0.05))` but if maxd smaller, d=maxd. For fine, maybe too large; bad accepted? SA can reject. Also try small: `scale = rng.choice([0.001,0.005,0.01,0.05])`; d=min(maxd, scale). Good.

SA may be slow; limit iterations by time.

Subgradient pass:
```
def subgradient_pass(h,c,M,deadline,rng,K=8,max_iters=100):
  n=len(h); target=n*0.5; best...
  for it in range(max_iters):
    if time>deadline: break
    active=top_active(c,K)
    G=np.zeros(n)
    for idx in active: G += grad_lag(h, idx-(n-1))
    G -= np.mean(G)
    gmax=np.max(np.abs(G))
    if gmax < 1e-12: break
    improved=False
    for alpha in [0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001]:
       y=project_box_sum(h - (alpha/gmax)*G, target)
       cnew=recompute_raw(y); Mnew=max(cnew)
       if Mnew < M - 1e-12:
          h=y;c=cnew;M=Mnew; updated best; improved=True; break
    if not improved: break
  return h,c,M
```
But if M currently from c (recomputed), y maybe not sum exact? project. Good.
Use `alpha/gmax` where alpha up to0.5 gives step*G ~0.5 for max gradient. Might be too aggressive; line search exact. Good.

Potential issue: G raw sum of top K gradients. We subtract mean to enforce sum; projection also enforces. Good.

Initial random fourier design:
```
def random_fourier_init(n, rng, beta=None):
    t = (np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
    s = np.zeros(n)
    nf = rng.randint(2,6)
    for _ in range(nf):
        f = rng.randint(1, 12)
        phase = 2*np.pi*rng.random()
        amp = rng.uniform(0.5,1.5)
        s += amp*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*f*t/2.0 + phase) # period 1? Since t in [0,2], 2π f t /2 = π f t: f cycles over length2.
    if beta is None: beta = rng.choice([5,10,20,40])
    h = 1.0/(1.0+np.exp(-beta*s))
    return project_box_sum(h,n*0.5)
```
This yields blocky periodic, sum adjusted.
Also random binary:
```
def random_binary_init(n,rng):
   h=np.zeros(n); idx=rng.choice(n, size=n//2, replace=False); h[idx]=1; return h
```

Symmetries in deep:
```
def generate_variants(h, rng):
   candidates=[]
   rev=h[::-1]
   candidates.append(0.5*(h+rev))
   candidates.append(0.5*(h+1-rev))
   for sh in [1, max(1,n//4), n//2]: candidates.append(0.5*(h+np.roll(h,sh)))
   candidates.append(binary_from_h(h))
   # maybe threshold with slightly different quantile: choose top n/2 values.
   return candidates
```
For anti-palindromic: `0.5*(h + (1 - rev))` sum = (sum h + n - sum rev)/2 = n/2. Good.
Evaluate each with `compute_c5_direct`; choose best variant as start if better than current. But local search might improve variant even if variant worse; we can include as separate quick hill if time. Deep can use best variant for first pass; then maybe if time, try second variant quick.

2. `transfer_e_vec(D,R,m,h)` formula: e = g_R - g_D.
`g_P` = first if P+m valid (1 - h[P+m]) + second if P-m valid (- h[P-m]). So:
e = [1-if R+m valid - h[R+m]] + [-if R-m valid h[R-m]]
   - [1-if D+m valid - h[D+m]] + [if D-m valid h[D-m]]? Wait subtract g_D: g_D = firstD + secondD = (1-h[D+m]) - h[D-m]. e = g_R - g_D = (1-h[R+m]) - h[R-m] - (1-h[D+m]) + h[D-m]. In code:
- e += (1-h[R+m]) for firstR
- e -= h[R-m]
- e -= (1-h[D+m])
- e += h[D-m]
Matches.
Test positive lag m>0, D=i,R=i+m. R+m invalid; R-m=D valid -> e -= h[D]. D+m=R valid -> e -= (1-h[R]). D-m invalid. e = -h_D - (1-h_R) = h_R -1 -h_D. Earlier. Good. If D low/R high? okay.
Lag0: R+m=R valid: 1-h_R; R-m=R: -h_R; sum g_R=1-2h_R. D similarly; e=2h_D-2h_R? Wait 1-2h_R -(1-2h_D)=2h_D-2h_R. This is g_R-g_D. Transfer D->R change d*e. To reduce C0 (concave), need move from low D to high R: h_D<h_R -> e negative. Good.

Now, potential performance of `project_box_sum` bisection inside subgradient many times. Could optimize by first if y within bounds and sum off small: A faster projection:
```
def project_box_sum(y,target):
  y=np.clip(y,0,1)
  s=y.sum(); diff=target-s
  if abs(diff) < 1e-10: fix exact; return
  # If y has many interior, simple iterative? Bisection robust.
```

But in final, `project_box_sum` final exact adjustment with `x[idx]=...` may make sum exact but if diff not tiny? Bisection should make tiny. If not (e.g., target infeasible? no), adjust may clip. Good.

Let's implement exact adjustment robust:
After bisection, `s = x.sum(); diff = target - s`.
If abs(diff)>1e-14:

   add = min(rem, -x[idx]? no room)
   x[idx] += add; rem -= add
```

```

Detailed run flow:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
  global _USE_FFT
  t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  _USE_FFT = _validate_corr()
  best_h=None; best_c5=inf; best_n=0
  # parent
  parent_h=None
  try:
    if 'initial_h_values' in globals():
      parent_h=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy()
      n_par=len(parent_h)
      if n_par>MAX_N: parent_h=resample_h(parent_h,MAX_N); n_par=MAX_N
      if n_par<2: parent_h=np.full(MAX_N,0.5)
      parent_h=project_box_sum(parent_h,n_par*0.5)
      c5=compute_c5_direct(parent_h)
      update best
  except: pass
  if parent_h is None:
      parent_h=random_fourier_init(500,rng); c5=...; update
  # Exploration
  explore_deadline=min(deadline, t0 + min(220, budget_s*0.22))
  ns=...
  for n in ns:
     if time>explore_deadline: break
     remaining = explore_deadline - time; count left
     slice = max(6, min(20, remaining / max(1, len(ns) - processed +1)))
     # start parent resample
     if parent_h is not None:
       h = resample_h(parent_h,n)
     else: h=random_fourier_init(n,rng)
     # if n == parent_n maybe skip? But best parent already; we can still quick if slice enough? skip n==best_n? maybe if n==parent_n continue to use time deep.
     if len(h)==best_n and abs? skip if n==parent_n? To save, skip if n == len(parent_h) and parent exists.
     h,_,_ = quick_optimize(h, min(explore_deadline, time+slice), rng)
     c5=compute_c5_direct(h); update
     if remaining>8 and n<=256:
       h2 = random_binary_init(n,rng)
       h2,_,_ = quick_optimize(h2, min(explore_deadline, time+6), rng)
       update
       h3 = random_fourier_init(n,rng)
       if time<explore_deadline: quick 6s update
  # Deep
  if time < deadline:
     h = best_h.copy()
     # variants
     t_var = min(deadline, time+30)
     best_var_h = h; best_var_c = best_c5
     for var in variants:
        if time>t_var: break
        vv=project_box_sum(var,len(var)); c5=compute_c5_direct(vv)
        if c5<best_var_c: best_var_h=vv; best_var_c=c5
     if best_var_h is not h: h=best_var_h; best_c5=...; best_h=h
     # Maybe quick optimize variants if time? skip
     # advanced local search slice deadline - 250 (reserve)
     local_deadline = min(deadline, time + max(120, (deadline - time)*0.55))
     h, raw, Mraw = local_search_advanced(h, local_deadline, rng, K=8, slice_s=25?)
     c5=compute_c5_direct(h); update
     # SA
     if time < deadline - 30:
        dx=2.0/len(h); raw=recompute_raw(h); M=max
        sa_deadline=min(deadline-20, time + 120)
        h2,M2=simulated_annealing(h,raw,M,dx,sa_deadline,rng)
        c5=compute_c5_direct(h2); update
     # Random restarts at best_n if time
     if time < deadline - 60:
        n1 = len(best_h)
        for start_type in range(4):
           if time > deadline - 40: break
           if start_type==0: h=rng normal around best
           elif: random_fourier_init(n1)
           elif: binary_from_h(best_h)
           else: sym variant
           slice=min(45, (deadline-40)/ (remaining))
           h,_,_ = quick_optimize(h, min(deadline-20,time+slice), rng)
           update
     # Final polish best
     if time < deadline - 10:
        final_deadline=deadline-3
        h=best_h; h,raw,M=local_search_advanced(h, final_deadline, rng, K=8, slice_s=10, step_list small?) maybe sweep small steps only.
        c5=compute_c5_direct(h)
  # ensure project and direct
  if best_h is None: fallback parent or constant
  best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, len(best_h)*0.5)
  best_c5 = compute_c5_direct(best_h)
  return best_h, best_c5, len(best_h)
```

Let's write `sweep_step` code carefully:
```
def sweep_step(h, c, M, deadline, step, rng, K=8, max_passes=500):
    n=len(h); tol=1e-11
    best_M=M
    improved_total=False
    for pass_idx in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        # recompute
        c = recompute_raw(h)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M < best_M: best_M=M
        active = top_active(c, K)
        filter_gap = max(1e-8, 1e-4*(M+1.0))
        act_idx = [int(ix) for ix in active if c[ix] >= M - filter_gap]
        if not act_idx: act_idx = [int(active[0])]
        e_tol=1e-7
        pass_improved=False
        improved_in_pass=0
        for prim_idx in act_idx:  # Note: act_idx from c before pass; if improvements, active lags maybe stale. But pass loop fixed; okay.
            if time.time() > deadline: break
            prim_lag = prim_idx - (n-1)
            if prim_lag == 0:
                # random low-to-high pairs, perhaps 200 candidates
                cand_count = min(500, max(50, n//2))
                for _ in range(cand_count):
                    if time.time() > deadline: break
                    p = rng.randint(0,n); q = rng.randint(0,n); if p==q continue
                    if h[p] < h[q]: D=p; R=q
                    else: D=q; R=p
                    if abs(h[D]-h[R]) < 1e-10: continue
                    maxd = min(h[D], 1-h[R]); if maxd<=1e-12 continue
                    # filter against act_idx? For random, can do simple e check loop.
                    ok=True
                    for li in act_idx:
                       lag = li-(n-1)
                       e = point_coeff_scalar(R,lag,h) - point_coeff_scalar(D,lag,h)
                       if e > e_tol: ok=False; break
                    if not ok: continue
                    base = min(step, maxd)
                    if base <= 1e-12: continue
                    # exact test
                    accepted = try_amounts(h,c,D,R,base,M,tol,step)
                    if accepted:
                       M = float(np.max(c))
                       pass_improved=True; improved_in_pass+=1
                       if improved_in_pass>=50: break
                continue
            ma = abs(prim_lag)
            if ma >= n: continue
            if prim_lag > 0:
                count = n - ma
                D = np.arange(count)
                R = D + ma
            else:
                count = n - ma
                D = np.arange(ma, n)  # length n-ma
                R = D - ma
            maxd = np.minimum(h[D], 1.0 - h[R])
            allowed = maxd > 1e-12
            if not np.any(allowed): continue
            e_max = None
            for li in act_idx:
                lag = li - (n-1)
                e = transfer_e_vec(D, R, lag, h)
                if e_max is None:
                    e_max = e.copy()
                else:
                    np.maximum(e_max, e, out=e_max)
                allowed &= (e <= e_tol)
            if not np.any(allowed): continue
            allowed &= (e_max < -1e-10)
            cand = np.flatnonzero(allowed)
            if cand.size == 0: continue
            # score
            score = np.zeros(count)
            # maybe compute for allowed only
            score Allowed? Need array length count. Use maxd * (-e_max), but e_max maybe None? not.
            score = np.where(allowed, maxd * (-e_max), 0.0)
            # If too many, choose top
            max_cand = 400
            if cand.size > max_cand:
                sc = score[cand]
                # if step small, random subset? Use top by score plus random
                topn = min(max_cand, cand.size)
                part = np.argpartition(sc, -topn)[-topn:]
                cand = cand[part]
            # order: descending score, but randomize ties maybe
            order = np.argsort(-score[cand])
            cand = cand[order]
            accepted_primary=0
            for pos in cand:
                if (pos & 63) == 0 and time.time() > deadline: break
                maxd_i = maxd[pos]
                if maxd_i <= 1e-12: continue
                # Since h may changed after previous accepts, maxd_i stale. Recompute current donor/receiver:
                d_i = int(D[pos]); r_i=int(R[pos])
                cur_maxd = min(h[d_i], 1.0 - h[r_i])
                if cur_maxd <= 1e-12: continue
                # Re-check filter? h changed; could be stale. We can skip filter and rely exact, but may test many. Maybe quick scalar re-filter for act_idx if accepted_primary>0?
                if accepted_primary > 0:
                    ok=True
                    for li in act_idx:
                       lag=li-(n-1)
                       e=point_coeff_scalar(r_i,lag,h)-point_coeff_scalar(d_i,lag,h)
                       if e > e_tol: ok=False; break
                    if not ok: continue
                base = min(step, cur_maxd)
                if base <= 1e-12: continue
                accepted = try_amounts(h,c,d_i,r_i,base,M,tol,step)
                if accepted:
                    M = float(np.max(c))
                    pass_improved=True
                    improved_in_pass += 1
                    accepted_primary += 1
                    if accepted_primary >= 50:
                        break
        if improved_in_pass == 0:
            break
        # if improved_in_pass small and pass_idx>20 maybe continue with time slice controls
    return h,c,M
```
Need helper `point_coeff_scalar(p,m,h)` and `try_amounts`.
`point_coeff_scalar`:
```
def point_coeff_scalar(p,m,h):
  n=len(h); val=0.0
  q=p+m
  if 0 <= q < n: val += 1.0 - h[q]
  q=p-m
  if 0 <= q < n: val += -h[q]
  return val
```
For m=0, first/second both same q=p, val=1-2h[p]. Good.

`try_amounts(h,c,d,r,base,M,tol,step)`:
```
def try_amounts(h,c,d,r,base,M,tol,step):
    factors=[1.0]
    if base > 0.02:
       factors=[1.0,0.5,0.25]
    elif base > 0.002:
       factors=[1.0,0.5]
    else: factors=[1.0]
    for f in factors:
       amt=base*f
       if amt<=1e-14: continue
       # clip
       maxpos = min(h[d], 1.0-h[r])
       if amt > maxpos: amt=maxpos
       if amt <= 1e-14: continue
       apply_transfer(h,c,d,r,amt)
       newM=float(np.max(c))
       if newM < M - tol:
          return True
       apply_transfer(h,c,r,d,amt)
    return False
```
Need `apply_transfer` top-level. `M` passed old; if first factor fails, revert, second uses new h restored. Good.
Tol raw: maybe 1e-11. If using raw M ~100, improvements 0.001; tol fine. Could use `tol = 1e-12`.

When accepted, `M` in sweep loop should be updated to newM. `try_amounts` doesn't return newM. It updates c and h. We can recompute newM as `float(np.max(c))` after. That's O(n) extra. Could return newM to avoid. Modify `try_amounts` returns `(accepted,newM)` or None. Top-level no closures.
```
def try_amounts(...):
  for...
    if newM < M - tol: return True, newM
  return False, M
```
Then in loop: `accepted, M_new = try_amounts; if accepted: M=M_new;`.

For random lag0, after accept, M update.

In vector pass, after accepted_primary >0, re-filter uses current h; but if many accepts, old `e_max` invalid for remaining candidates; re-filter each candidate more expensive (K scalar). But accepted_primary break at 50, so within same primary max 50 accepts. After 50, break to next pass recompute. Good. For candidates after a few accepts, re-filter. If no accepts yet, use vector pre-filter. Good.

But `accepted_primary` counts accepts for this primary; if M improved but other primary lags stale. Fine.

`point_coeff_scalar` in re-filter loops act_idx length maybe small. Good.

Potential bug: In vector section, `score = np.where(allowed, maxd * (-e_max), 0.0)`. If e_max array length count. Good. If allowed false but e_max positive, score 0. `np.argsort(-score[cand])`. If scores all zero? allowed ensured e_max<-1e-10, and maxd>eps, score positive.

Now, `subgradient_pass` uses `project_box_sum`, `recompute_raw`. It tracks best. It may be called after each step with deadline slice small. Implement:
```
def subgradient_pass(h,c,M,deadline,rng,K=8,max_iters=200):
   n=len(h); target=n*0.5; best_M=M; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
   for _ in range(max_iters):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      # ensure M from c? use passed
      active=top_active(c,K)
      G=np.zeros(n)
      for idx in active:
         G += grad_lag(h, idx-(n-1))
      G -= np.mean(G)
      gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(G)))
      if gmax < 1e-12: break
      improved=False
      for alpha in (0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001):
         y=project_box_sum(h - (alpha/gmax)*G, target)
         c2=recompute_raw(y)
         M2=float(np.max(c2))
         if M2 < M - 1e-11:
             h=y; c=c2; M=M2; improved=True
             break
      if not improved:
         break
      if M < best_M:
         best_M=M; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
   return best_h,best_c,best_M
```
But if M current not recomputed at start? It is. If accepts, M lower. At next iteration uses active from new c. Good.

Should use top K but weights? Uniform. Could use reverse gap weights:
```
vals=c[active]; scale=max(1e-12, vals[0]-vals[-1]); weights=np.exp((vals - vals[0])/scale)...
```
Uniform simpler. Could weight top more: `weights = 1/(rank+1)`. But uniform okay.

`grad_lag` for m negative:
```
def grad_lag(h,m):
 n=len(h); g=np.zeros(n)
 if m==0: g = 1.0 - 2.0*h (copy? if h int, new array)
 elif m>0:
   g[0:n-m] += 1.0 - h[m:n]
   g[m:n] -= h[0:n-m]
 else:
   p=-m
   g[p:n] += 1.0 - h[0:n-p]
   g[0:n-p] -= h[p:n]
 return g
```
Need for m=0 return `1.0 - 2.0*h` creates new array. Good.

Run detailed code:
```
# after exploration
if time.time() < deadline - 30:
   h_cur = best_h.copy()
   # variants: allocate 20s
   t_var = min(deadline - 30, time.time()+25)
   bestc=best_c5
   for var in generate_variants(h_cur):
      if time>t_var: break
      vv=project_box_sum(var, len(var)*0.5)
      c5=compute_c5_direct(vv)
      if c5<bestc:
         bestc=c5; best_h=vv.copy(); best_n=len(vv); h_cur=vv.copy()
   # local search
   if time.time() < deadline - 40:
      R = deadline - time.time()
      local_dl = min(deadline, time.time() + max(30, R*0.55))
      h_cur, _, _ = local_search_advanced(h_cur, local_dl, rng, K=8, slice_s=20)
      c5=compute_c5_direct(h_cur); update
   # SA
   if time.time() < deadline - 40:
      R = deadline - time.time()
      sa_dl = min(deadline - 20, time.time() + max(30, min(150, R*0.3)))
      dx=2.0/len(best_h); raw=recompute_raw(best_h); M=max(raw)
      h_sa,_ = simulated_annealing(best_h, raw, M, dx, sa_dl, rng)
      c5=compute_c5_direct(h_sa); update
   # random restarts
   if time.time() < deadline - 60:
      nstart=len(best_h)
      attempts=4
      for a in range(attempts):
         rem=deadline - 60 - time.time()
         if rem<20: break
         slice=min(45, rem/(attempts-a))
         if a==0: h=best_h + rng.normal(0,0.02,nstart); h=project...
         elif a==1: h=random_fourier_init(nstart,rng)
         elif a==2: h=binary_from_h(best_h)
         else: h=generate_variants... choose maybe anti
         h,_,_ = quick_optimize(h, time.time()+slice, rng)
         c5=... update
   # final polish
   if time.time() < deadline - 8:

```
def random_fourier_init(n, rng, beta=None):
    t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    s=np.zeros(n)
    nf=rng.randint(2,6)
    for _ in range(nf):
        f=rng.randint(1,13)
        phase=2.0*np.pi*rng.random()
        amp=rng.uniform(0.4,1.2)
        s += amp*np.sin(np.pi*f*t + phase) # f cycles over [0,2]
    s=np.clip(s,-10,10)
    if beta is None: beta=float(rng.choice([5,8,12,20,35]))
    h=1.0/(1.0+np.exp(-beta*s))
    return project_box_sum(h, 0.5*n)
```
`rng.choice` with list needs 1D? Use `float(rng.choice([5,8,12,20,35]))` okay but choice may return array scalar. Use `float(rng.random()...)`? Fine. Better: `beta = [5,8,12,20,35][rng.randint(0,5)]`.

Loop:
```
max_cycles=8
for cycle in range(max_cycles):
    if time.time()>=deadline: break
    cycle_improved=False
    for step in steps:
       if time.time()>=deadline: break
       step_dl=min(deadline, time.time()+slice_s)
       if M > best_M + 1e-9: h=best_h; c=best_c; M=best_M
       h,c,M = sweep_step(h,c,M,step_dl,step,rng,K=K)
       if M < best_M - 1e-11:
           best_M=M; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); cycle_improved=True
       if do_subgrad and time.time() < deadline - 5:
           sub_dl=min(deadline, time.time()+5)
           h2,c2,M2=subgradient_pass(best_h,best_c,best_M,sub_dl,rng,K=K)
           if M2 < best_M -1e-11:
               best_M=M2; best_h=h2; best_c=c2; h=best_h;c=best_c;M=best_M; cycle_improved=True
    if not cycle_improved: break
return best_h,best_c,best_M
```

```
def project_box_sum(y, target_sum):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    n = y.size
    if n == 0: return y.copy()
    x = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if abs(s - target_sum) > 1e-12:
        lo = float(x.min()) - 1.0
        hi = float(x.max()) + 1.0
        for _ in range(80):
            mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
            z = np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
            sz = float(z.sum())
            if sz > target_sum:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        x = np.clip(x - 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    # final exact corrections
    for _ in range(3):
        s = float(x.sum())
        diff = target_sum - s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - x
            idx = int(np.argmax(room))
            add = min(diff, room[idx])
            if add != 0:
                x[idx] += add
        else:
            room = x
            idx = int(np.argmin(x))
            sub = min(-diff, room[idx])
            if sub != 0:
                x[idx] -= sub
    # if still not, choose interior and set exact
    s = float(x.sum()); diff=target_sum-s
    if abs(diff)>0:
        interior = np.where((x > 1e-15) & (x < 1.0 - 1e-15))[0]
        if interior.size == 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(x * (1.0 - x)))
        else:
            idx = int(interior[np.argmax(x[interior]*(1.0-x[interior]))])
        val = float(x[idx] + diff)
        if val < 0.0: val=0.0
        elif val > 1.0: val=1.0
        x[idx] = val
    return x
```
The repeated one-by-one only up to 3, diff tiny. If diff not tiny, min diff but only one idx; after 3 may not fix if diff large. But bisection should. To be safer, distribute before final:
After bisection, if abs(diff)>1e-8, do room distribution:
```
if diff>0: room=1-x; order=argsort(-room); rem=diff; for idx in order: add=min(rem,room[idx]); if add>0: x[idx]+=add; rem-=add; if rem==0 break
```
This O(n log n) only when large. Use. But final tiny. Keep simpler? For subgradient, y may be far from target; bisection handles. No need distribution.

Maybe if target=n/2 and y all 0? Bisection returns x with 0.5? lo=-1,hi=1. For target 250, sum target feasible. Works.

def simulated_annealing(h, raw, M, dx, deadline, rng, K=8, T0=1e-4, Tmin=1e-7, cool=0.9998):
   n=len(h); h=h.copy(); c=raw.copy(); M=float(M)
   best_M=M; best_h=h.copy()
   T=T0; no_improve=0; active=top_active(c,K); stale=0
   scales = [0.0005,0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.25]
   while time.time()<deadline and T>Tmin:
      if ...
      if stale >= 20:
         active = top_active(c,K); stale=0
      r=rand
      d=r; rec=r; maxd=0
      if r < 0.8 and len(active)>0:
         idx = active[rng.randint(len(active))]
         lag = int(idx - (n-1))
         if lag == 0:
            p=... q=...
            if h[p] < h[q]: d=p;rec=q else d=q;rec=p
         else:
            ma=abs(lag); if ma>=n: continue
            i=rng.randint(n-ma)
            if lag>0: d=i; rec=i+ma
            else: d=i+ma; rec=i
         maxd = min(h[d], 1-h[rec])
      else:
         p=... q=...
         if p==q or rng.random()<0.05: continue
         if h[p] < h[q]: d=p; rec=q else d=q; rec=p
         maxd=min(h[d],1-h[rec])
      if maxd <=1e-14: T*=cool; continue
      scale=scales[rng.randint(len(scales))]
      amt = min(maxd, scale)
      if amt < 1e-14: ...
      apply_transfer(...amt); newM=max(c); delta=(newM-M)*dx
      accept=False
      if newM < M - 1e-12: accept=True
      elif T > 0:
         ratio = delta/T if max(delta,0)>0 else 0
         if ratio < 50 and rng.random() < math.exp(-ratio): accept=True
      if accept:
         M=newM
         if M < best_M - 1e-12:
            best_M=M; best_h=h.copy(); no_improve=0
         else: no_improve += 1
      else:
         apply_transfer(...,rec,d,amt)
         no_improve += 1
      stale += 1
      T *= cool
      if no_improve > 3000:
         h = best_h + rng.normal(0, 0.02, n)
         h = project_box_sum(h,n*0.5)
         c = recompute_raw(h); M = float(np.max(c)); no_improve=0; T=max(T,1e-5); active=top_active(c,K); stale=0
   return best_h,best_M
```

When skip due rng, T update.

```python
import numpy as np
import time

_USE_FFT = True

_DEEP_STEPS = [
    0.50, 0.25, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03, 0.015,
    0.008, 0.004, 0.002, 0.001,
    0.0005, 0.00025, 0.00012
]
_QUICK_STEPS = [
    0.25, 0.10, 0.04, 0.012, 0.004, 0.001
]
_FINAL_STEPS = [
    0.004, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0005, 0.0002, 0.0001
]


def full_corr_fft(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(h[0]) * (1.0 - h[0])], dtype=float)
    v = 1.0 - h
    m = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1 << (m).bit_length()
    H = np.fft.fft(h, L)
    V = np.fft.fft(v, L)
    r = np.fft.ifft(H * np.conj(V)).real
    out = np.empty(m, dtype=float)
    out[:n - 1] = r[L - (n - 1):]
    out[n - 1:] = r[:n]
    out[np.abs(out) < 1e-14] = 0.0
    return out


def validate_corr_fft():
    try:
        n = 17
        h = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) % 7) / 7.0
        h = np.clip(0.2 + 0.8 * h, 0.0, 1.0)
        h = project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * n)
        a = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        b = full_corr_fft(h)
        return bool(np.allclose(a, b, rtol=1e-10, atol=1e-10))
    except Exception:
        return False


def recompute_raw(h):
    if _USE_FFT:
        return full_corr_fft(h)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def compute_c5_direct(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.0
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)


def project_box_sum(y, target_sum):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    n = y.size
    if n == 0:
        return y.copy()
    x = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)

    s = float(x.sum())
    if abs(s - target_sum) > 1e-12:
        lo = float(x.min()) - 1.0
        hi = float(x.max()) + 1.0
        for _ in range(80):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            z = np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
            sz = float(z.sum())
            if sz > target_sum:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        x = np.clip(x - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

    # Small exact correction.
    for _ in range(3):
        s = float(x.sum())
        diff = target_sum - s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - x
            idx = int(np.argmax(room))
            add = min(diff, float(room[idx]))
            if add > 0.0:
                x[idx] += add
        else:
            room = x
            idx = int(np.argmin(x))
            sub = min(-diff, float(room[idx]))
            if sub > 0.0:
                x[idx] -= sub

    s = float(x.sum())
    diff = target_sum - s
    if abs(diff) > 0.0:
        interior = np.where((x > 1e-15) & (x < 1.0 - 1e-15))[0]
        if interior.size == 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(x * (1.0 - x)))
        else:
            w = x[interior] * (1.0 - x[interior])
            idx = int(interior[int(np.argmax(w))])
        val = float(x[idx] + diff)
        if val < 0.0:
            val = 0.0
        elif val > 1.0:
            val = 1.0
        x[idx] = val
    return x


def top_active(c, K):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    ell = c.size
    if ell == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    K = int(min(K, ell))
    if K == ell:
        idx = np.arange(ell)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    idx = idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]]
    return idx.astype(int, copy=False)


def apply_point_delta(h, c, p, delta):
    if delta == 0.0:
        return
    n = len(h)

    # Terms where p is the first factor h_p.
    cnt = n - p
    if cnt > 0:
        c[n - 1:n - 1 + cnt] += delta * (1.0 - h[p:p + cnt])

    # Terms where p is the second factor (1 - h_p).
    if p >= 0:
        c[n - 1 + p:n - 2:-1] += -delta * h[p::-1]

    h[p] += delta
    if h[p] < 0.0:
        h[p] = 0.0
    elif h[p] > 1.0:
        h[p] = 1.0


def apply_transfer(h, c, donor, receiver, amount):
    if donor == receiver or amount == 0.0:
        return
    apply_point_delta(h, c, donor, -amount)
    apply_point_delta(h, c, receiver, amount)


def point_coeff_scalar(p, m, h):
    n = len(h)
    val = 0.0
    q = p + m
    if 0 <= q < n:
        val += 1.0 - h[q]
    q = p - m
    if 0 <= q < n:
        val += -h[q]
    return val


def transfer_e_vec(D, R, m, h):
    n = len(h)
    e = np.zeros(len(D), dtype=float)

    qx = R + m
    good = (qx >= 0) & (qx < n)
    if np.any(good):
        e[good] += 1.0 - h[qx[good]]

    qx = R - m
    good = (qx >= 0) & (qx < n)
    if np.any(good):
        e[good] -= h[qx[good]]

    qx = D + m
    good = (qx >= 0) & (qx < n)
    if np.any(good):
        e[good] -= (1.0 - h[qx[good]])

    qx = D - m
    good = (qx >= 0) & (qx < n)
    if np.any(good):
        e[good] += h[qx[good]]

    return e


def try_single_amount(h, c, donor, receiver, amt, M, tol):
    if amt <= 1e-14:
        return False, M
    maxpos = min(float(h[donor]), float(1.0 - h[receiver]))
    if amt > maxpos:
        amt = maxpos
    if amt <= 1e-14:
        return False, M

    apply_transfer(h, c, donor, receiver, amt)
    newM = float(np.max(c))
    if newM < M - tol:
        return True, newM

    apply_transfer(h, c, receiver, donor, amt)
    return False, M


def grad_lag(h, m):
    n = len(h)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if m == 0:
        g = 1.0 - 2.0 * h
    elif m > 0:
        n2 = n - m
        g[0:n2] += 1.0 - h[m:n]
        g[m:n] -= h[0:n2]
    else:
        p = -m
        n2 = n - p
        g[p:n] += 1.0 - h[0:n2]
        g[0:n2] -= h[p:n]
    return g


def sweep_step(h, c, M, deadline, step, rng, K=8, max_passes=1000):
    n = len(h)
    tol = 1e-11

    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break

        c = recompute_raw(h)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        active = top_active(c, K)
        if active.size == 0:
            break

        filter_gap = max(1e-8, 1e-4 * (float(M) + 1.0))
        act_idx = [int(i) for i in active if c[i] >= M - filter_gap]
        if not act_idx:
            act_idx = [int(active[0])]
        e_tol = 1e-7

        improved_in_pass = 0

        for prim_idx in act_idx:
            if time.time() > deadline or improved_in_pass > 120:
                break

            prim_lag = int(prim_idx - (n - 1))

            if prim_lag == 0:
                # Push mass from low values to high values to reduce lag 0.
                max_pairs = min(220, max(20, n // 4))
                if max_pairs > 0:
                    ord_h = np.argsort(h)
                    lo = ord_h[:max_pairs]
                    hi = ord_h[::-1][:max_pairs]
                    nm = min(lo.size, hi.size)
                    for j in range(nm):
                        if (j & 63) == 0 and time.time() > deadline:
                            break
                        d_i = int(lo[j])
                        r_i = int(hi[nm - 1 - j])
                        if d_i == r_i:
                            continue
                        if h[d_i] >= h[r_i] - 1e-12:
                            continue
                        maxd = min(float(h[d_i]), float(1.0 - h[r_i]))
                        if maxd <= 1e-12:
                            continue

                        ok = True
                        for li in act_idx:
                            lag = int(li - (n - 1))
                            e = point_coeff_scalar(r_i, lag, h) - point_coeff_scalar(d_i, lag, h)
                            if e > e_tol:
                                ok = False
                                break
                        if not ok:
                            continue

                        amt = min(step, maxd)
                        accepted, M = try_single_amount(h, c, d_i, r_i, amt, M, tol)
                        if accepted:
                            improved_in_pass += 1
                            if improved_in_pass > 120:
                                break
                continue

            ma = abs(prim_lag)
            if ma >= n:
                continue

            if prim_lag > 0:
                count = n - ma
                D = np.arange(count, dtype=int)
                R = D + ma
            else:
                count = n - ma
                D = np.arange(ma, n, dtype=int)
                R = D - ma

            maxd = np.minimum(h[D], 1.0 - h[R])
            allowed = maxd > 1e-12
            if not np.any(allowed):
                continue

            e_max = None
            for li in act_idx:
                lag = int(li - (n - 1))
                e = transfer_e_vec(D, R, lag, h)
                if e_max is None:
                    e_max = e.copy()
                else:
                    np.maximum(e_max, e, out=e_max)
                allowed &= (e <= e_tol)

            if not np.any(allowed):
                continue
            allowed &= (e_max < -1e-10)

            cand = np.flatnonzero(allowed)
            if cand.size == 0:
                continue

            if n < 400:
                max_cand = 700
            elif n < 700:
                max_cand = 400
            else:
                max_cand = 220

            if cand.size > max_cand:
                score = np.where(allowed, maxd * (-e_max), 0.0)
                sc = score[cand]
                part = np.argpartition(sc, -max_cand)[-max_cand:]
                cand = cand[part]

            # Descending score, but a little randomization if many candidates.
            if c.size < 5000 and cand.size <= 900:
                score = np.where(allowed, maxd * (-e_max), 0.0)
                order = np.argsort(-score[cand])
                cand = cand[order]
            else:
                cand = cand[rng.permutation(cand.size)]

            accepted_primary = 0
            for ci, pos in enumerate(cand):
                if ci and (ci & 63) == 0 and time.time() > deadline:
                    break

                d_i = int(D[pos])
                r_i = int(R[pos])
                cur_maxd = min(float(h[d_i]), float(1.0 - h[r_i]))
                if cur_maxd <= 1e-12:
                    continue

                if accepted_primary > 0:
                    ok = True
                    for li in act_idx:
                        lag = int(li - (n - 1))
                        e = point_coeff_scalar(r_i, lag, h) - point_coeff_scalar(d_i, lag, h)
                        if e > e_tol:
                            ok = False
                            break
                    if not ok:
                        continue

                amt = min(step, cur_maxd)
                accepted, M = try_single_amount(h, c, d_i, r_i, amt, M, tol)
                if accepted:
                    improved_in_pass += 1
                    accepted_primary += 1
                    if accepted_primary >= 45 or improved_in_pass > 120:
                        break

    return h, c, M


def subgradient_pass(h, c, M, deadline, rng, K=8, max_iters=300):
    n = len(h)
    target = 0.5 * n
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_M = float(M)

    for _ in range(max_iters):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break

        c = recompute_raw(h)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M > best_M + 1e-10:
            h = best_h.copy()
            c = best_c.copy()
            M = best_M

        active = top_active(c, K)
        if active.size == 0:
            break

        G = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        for idx in active:
            lag = int(idx - (n - 1))
            G += grad_lag(h, lag)

        G -= float(np.mean(G))
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(G)))
        if gmax < 1e-12:
            break

        improved = False
        for alpha in (0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001):
            y = project_box_sum(h - (alpha / gmax) * G, target)
            c2 = recompute_raw(y)
            M2 = float(np.max(c2))
            if M2 < M - 1e-11:
                h = y
                c = c2
                M = M2
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            break

        if M < best_M - 1e-12:
            best_M = M
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_c = c.copy()

    return best_h, best_c, best_M


def local_search_advanced(h, deadline, rng, K=8, steps=None, slice_s=18, do_subgrad=True):
    n = len(h)
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = project_box_sum(h, target)
    c = recompute_raw(h)
    M = float(np.max(c))

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_M = M

    if steps is None:
        steps = _DEEP_STEPS

    for cycle in range(12):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        cycle_improved = False

        for step in steps:
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break

            if M > best_M + 1e-10:
                h = best_h.copy()
                c = best_c.copy()
                M = best_M

            step_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + max(3.0, float(slice_s)))
            h, c, M = sweep_step(h, c, M, step_deadline, step, rng, K=K)

            if M < best_M - 1e-11:
                best_M = M
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_c = c.copy()
                cycle_improved = True

            if do_subgrad and time.time() < deadline - 5:
                sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + 5.0)
                h2, c2, M2 = subgradient_pass(best_h, best_c, best_M, sub_deadline, rng, K=K)
                if M2 < best_M - 1e-11:
                    best_M = M2
                    best_h = h2
                    best_c = c2
                    h = best_h.copy()
                    c = best_c.copy()
                    M = best_M
                    cycle_improved = True

        if not cycle_improved:
            break

    return best_h, best_c, best_M


def quick_optimize(h, deadline, rng, K=8):
    n = len(h)
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = project_box_sum(h, target)
    c = recompute_raw(h)
    M = float(np.max(c))

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_M = M

    if time.time() < deadline:
        sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + max(2.0, (deadline - time.time()) * 0.25))
        h2, c2, M2 = subgradient_pass(best_h, best_c, best_M, sub_deadline, rng, K=K)
        if M2 < best_M - 1e-11:
            best_M = M2
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2

    for step in _QUICK_STEPS:
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        if M > best_M + 1e-10:
            h = best_h.copy()
            c = best_c.copy()
            M = best_M
        step_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + 8.0)
        h, c, M = sweep_step(h, c, M, step_deadline, step, rng, K=K)
        if M < best_M - 1e-11:
            best_M = M
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_c = c.copy()

    if time.time() < deadline:
        sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + 3.0)
        h2, c2, M2 = subgradient_pass(best_h, best_c, best_M, sub_deadline, rng, K=K)
        if M2 < best_M - 1e-11:
            best_M = M2
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2

    return best_h, best_c, best_M


def simulated_annealing(h, raw, M, dx, deadline, rng, K=8,
                        T0=8e-5, Tmin=1e-7, cool=0.9998):
    n = len(h)
    h = h.copy()
    c = raw.copy()
    M = float(M)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_M = M

    T = float(T0)
    no_improve = 0
    active = top_active(c, K)
    stale = 0
    scales = [0.0005, 0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.25]

    while time.time() < deadline and T > Tmin:
        if stale >= 20:
            active = top_active(c, K)
            stale = 0

        rejected = False
        d_i = -1
        r_i = -1
        maxd = 0.0

        if active.size > 0 and rng.random() < 0.82:
            idx = int(active[rng.randint(active.size)])
            lag = int(idx - (n - 1))
            if lag == 0:
                p = int(rng.randint(n))
                q = int(rng.randint(n))
                if p != q:
                    if h[p] < h[q]:
                        d_i, r_i = p, q
                    else:
                        d_i, r_i = q, p
            else:
                ma = abs(lag)
                if ma < n:
                    i = int(rng.randint(n - ma))
                    if lag > 0:
                        d_i, r_i = i, i + ma
                    else:
                        d_i, r_i = i + ma, i
        else:
            p = int(rng.randint(n))
            q = int(rng.randint(n))
            if p != q:
                if h[p] < h[q]:
                    d_i, r_i = p, q
                else:
                    d_i, r_i = q, p

        if d_i < 0:
            T *= cool
            stale += 1
            no_improve += 1
            continue

        maxd = min(float(h[d_i]), float(1.0 - h[r_i]))
        if maxd <= 1e-13:
            T *= cool
            stale += 1
            no_improve += 1
            continue

        scale = float(scales[rng.randint(len(scales))])
        amt = min(maxd, scale)
        if amt <= 1e-14:
            T *= cool
            stale += 1
            no_improve += 1
            continue

        apply_transfer(h, c, d_i, r_i, amt)
        newM = float(np.max(c))
        delta = (newM - M) * dx

        accepted = False
        if newM < M - 1e-12:
            accepted = True
        else:
            ratio = delta / T if delta > 0.0 else 0.0
            if ratio < 50.0 and float(rng.random()) < float(np.exp(-ratio)):
                accepted = True

        if accepted:
            M = newM
            if M < best_M - 1e-12:
                best_M = float(M)
                best_h = h.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            apply_transfer(h, c, r_i, d_i, amt)
            no_improve += 1

        stale += 1
        T *= cool

        if no_improve > 2500:
            h = best_h + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)
            h = project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * n)
            c = recompute_raw(h)
            M = float(np.max(c))
            no_improve = 0
            T = max(T, 1e-5)
            active = top_active(c, K)
            stale = 0

    return best_h, best_M


def resample_h(h_src, n_dst):
    h_src = np.asarray(h_src, dtype=float)
    if len(h_src) == n_dst:
        return project_box_sum(h_src, 0.5 * n_dst)
    xs = (np.arange(len(h_src)) + 0.5) * (2.0 / len(h_src))
    xd = (np.arange(n_dst) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_dst)
    y = np.interp(xd, xs, h_src)
    return project_box_sum(y, 0.5 * n_dst)


def binary_from_h(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    target = int(round(0.5 * n))
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    idx = np.argsort(-h)[:target]
    out[idx] = 1.0
    return out


def random_fourier_init(n, rng, beta=None):
    n = int(n)
    t = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    s = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    nf = int(rng.randint(2, 6))
    for _ in range(nf):
        f = int(rng.randint(1, 14))
        phase = 2.0 * np.pi * float(rng.random())
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.35, 1.25))
        s += amp * np.sin(np.pi * float(f) * t + phase)
    s = np.clip(s, -10.0, 10.0)
    if beta is None:
        beta = [4.0, 6.0, 10.0, 16.0, 28.0, 45.0][int(rng.randint(0, 6))]
    h = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-float(beta) * s))
    return project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * n)


def random_binary_init(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    idx = rng.permutation(n)[:int(round(0.5 * n))]
    h[idx] = 1.0
    return h


def generate_variants(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    rev = h[::-1]
    out = []
    out.append(0.5 * (h + rev))
    out.append(0.5 * (h + 1.0 - rev))
    for sh in (1, max(1, n // 4), n // 2):
        if 0 < sh < n:
            out.append(0.5 * (h + np.roll(h, sh)))
    out.append(binary_from_h(h))
    b = binary_from_h(h)
    for sh in (1, max(1, n // 4)):
        if 0 < sh < n:
            out.append(0.5 * (b + np.roll(b, sh)))
    return out


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    global _USE_FFT

    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    _USE_FFT = validate_corr_fft()
    MAX_N = 800

    best_h = None
    best_c5 = float("inf")
    best_n = 0

    def update_best(h, force=False):
        nonlocal_flag_dummy = None
    # The inner function above is not allowed by the contract, so do manual updates below.

    parent_h = None
    try:
        arr = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
        if arr.size >= 2:
            npar = int(arr.size)
            if npar % 2 != 0:
                npar += 1
            if npar > MAX_N:
                arr = resample_h(arr, MAX_N)
                npar = MAX_N
            else:
                arr = resample_h(arr, npar)
                npar = int(arr.size)
            arr = project_box_sum(arr, 0.5 * npar)
            parent_h = arr
            c5 = compute_c5_direct(parent_h)
            best_h = parent_h.copy()
            best_c5 = c5
            best_n = npar
    except Exception:
        parent_h = None

    if parent_h is None:
        npar = 500
        parent_h = random_fourier_init(npar, rng)
        parent_h = project_box_sum(parent_h, 0.5 * npar)
        c5 = compute_c5_direct(parent_h)
        best_h = parent_h.copy()
        best_c5 = c5
        best_n = npar

    # Resolution exploration.
    exploration_time = min(240.0, max(25.0, 0.22 * float(budget_s)))
    explore_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + exploration_time)

    base_ns = [
        48, 64, 96, 128, 160, 192, 256, 320,
        384, 448, 512, 640, 768
    ]
    ns = sorted(set([best_n] + [x for x in base_ns if 2 <= x <= MAX_N and x % 2 == 0]))

    processed = 0
    total = max(1, len(ns))
    for ntry in ns:
        if time.time() >= explore_deadline:
            break
        if ntry == best_n:
            continue

        rem = explore_deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 4.0:
            break
        left = max(1, total - processed)
        slice_s = max(5.0, min(16.0, rem / float(left)))
        processed += 1

        if parent_h is not None:
            hstart = resample_h(parent_h, ntry)
        else:
            hstart = random_fourier_init(ntry, rng)

        h1, _, _ = quick_optimize(hstart, min(explore_deadline, time.time() + slice_s), rng)
        c5 = compute_c5_direct(h1)
        if c5 < best_c5 - 1e-15:
            best_h = h1.copy()
            best_c5 = c5
            best_n = len(h1)

        if time.time() < explore_deadline - 4.0 and ntry <= 288:
            h2 = random_binary_init(ntry, rng)
            slice2 = min(6.0, max(2.0, (explore_deadline - time.time()) * 0.5))
            h2, _, _ = quick_optimize(h2, min(explore_deadline, time.time() + slice2), rng)
            c5 = compute_c5_direct(h2)
            if c5 < best_c5 - 1e-15:
                best_h = h2.copy()
                best_c5 = c5
                best_n = len(h2)

            if time.time() < explore_deadline - 3.0:
                h3 = random_fourier_init(ntry, rng)
                slice3 = min(5.0, max(2.0, (explore_deadline - time.time()) * 0.5))
                h3, _, _ = quick_optimize(h3, min(explore_deadline, time.time() + slice3), rng)
                c5 = compute_c5_direct(h3)
                if c5 < best_c5 - 1e-15:
                    best_h = h3.copy()
                    best_c5 = c5
                    best_n = len(h3)

    # Deep polish best candidate and variants.
    if time.time() < deadline - 30.0:
        hcur = best_h.copy()
        tvar = min(deadline - 30.0, time.time() + 25.0)
        variants = generate_variants(hcur)
        for var in variants:
            if time.time() > tvar:
                break
            vv = project_box_sum(var, 0.5 * len(var))
            c5 = compute_c5_direct(vv)
            if c5 < best_c5 - 1e-15:
                best_h = vv.copy()
                best_c5 = c5
                best_n = len(vv)
                hcur = vv.copy()

        if time.time() < deadline - 40.0:
            R = deadline - time.time()
            local_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + max(40.0, 0.55 * R))
            hcur, _, _ = local_search_advanced(
                hcur, local_deadline, rng, K=8, steps=_DEEP_STEPS, slice_s=16.0, do_subgrad=True
            )
            c5 = compute_c5_direct(hcur)
            if c5 < best_c5 - 1e-15:
                best_h = hcur.copy()
                best_c5 = c5
                best_n = len(hcur)

        if time.time() < deadline - 40.0:
            R = deadline - time.time()
            sa_deadline = min(deadline - 25.0, time.time() + max(30.0, min(160.0, 0.30 * R)))
            dx = 2.0 / float(len(best_h))
            raw = recompute_raw(best_h)
            M = float(np.max(raw))
            hsa, _ = simulated_annealing(best_h, raw, M, dx, sa_deadline, rng, K=8)
            c5 = compute_c5_direct(hsa)
            if c5 < best_c5 - 1e-15:
                best_h = hsa.copy()
                best_c5 = c5
                best_n = len(hsa)

        # Random restarts around best resolution.
        if time.time() < deadline - 50.0:
            nstart = int(best_n)
            rem = deadline - 50.0 - time.time()
            attempts = 4
            if nstart <= 2:
                attempts = 0
            attempts = min(attempts, max(0, int(rem // 20.0)))
            for a in range(attempts):
                if time.time() > deadline - 40.0:
                    break
                remaining_restarts = max(1, attempts - a)
                slice_r = min(45.0, max(12.0, (deadline - 50.0 - time.time()) / float(remaining_restarts)))

                if a == 0:
                    hr = best_h + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, nstart)
                    hr = project_box_sum(hr, 0.5 * nstart)
                elif a == 1:
                    hr = random_fourier_init(nstart, rng)
                elif a == 2:
                    hr = binary_from_h(best_h)
                else:
                    vr = generate_variants(best_h)
                    hr = vr[rng.randint(len(vr))]
                    hr = project_box_sum(hr, 0.5 * len(hr))
                    if len(hr) != nstart:
                        hr = resample_h(hr, nstart)

                hr, _, _ = quick_optimize(hr, min(deadline - 40.0, time.time() + slice_r), rng)
                c5 = compute_c5_direct(hr)
                if c5 < best_c5 - 1e-15:
                    best_h = hr.copy()
                    best_c5 = c5
                    best_n = len(hr)

        # Final fine polish.
        if time.time() < deadline - 10.0:
            hfin = best_h.copy()
            final_deadline = deadline - 3.0
            hfin, _, _ = local_search_advanced(
                hfin, final_deadline, rng, K=8, steps=_FINAL_STEPS, slice_s=8.0, do_subgrad=False
            )
            c5 = compute_c5_direct(hfin)
            if c5 < best_c5 - 1e-15:
                best_h = hfin.copy()
                best_c5 = c5
                best_n = len(hfin)

    if best_h is None:
        best_h = project_box_sum(np.full(500, 0.5), 250.0)
        best_c5 = compute_c5_direct(best_h)
        best_n = 500

    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, 0.5 * len(best_h))
    best_c5 = compute_c5_direct(best_h)
    return best_h, float(best_c5), int(len(best_h))
```
